so from (
A.4
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\boldsymbol{b}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t,s}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=s+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{b}_{t}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,q-1})\right\|_{M}

(A.6)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}|\boldsymbol{b}_{t}|^{2}_{2}}\times\sum_{q=s+1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{(1+s)^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}|\boldsymbol{b}_{t}|^{2}_{2}},

which proves the second result in (
A.2
).
∎

Corollary A.2
.

According to (
A.2
), for any
s\geq 0,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\boldsymbol{b}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,s}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\boldsymbol{b}_{t}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t,s})\right\|_{M}

(A.7)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\boldsymbol{b}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}\right\|_{M}+\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\boldsymbol{b}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t,s}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}|\mathbf{b}_{t}|^{2}_{2}}+\frac{C}{(1+s)^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}|\mathbf{b}_{t}|^{2}_{2}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}|\boldsymbol{b}_{t}|^{2}_{2}}.

The proof of Theorem
14
is based on the following decomposition: Suppose
t_{1}>t_{2},
then

\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}=\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}+\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\top}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}.

Since
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is measurable in the
\sigma
-field
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1},

\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}},
implying that
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\top}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right].
According to Lemma
A.2
,

\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}^{\top}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\mathbf{b}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{C|\mathbf{b}|_{2}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\quad\text{for any vector }\mathbf{b}\in\mathbf{R}^{d}.

Therefore, despite the fact that
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}
may exhibit complex dependence, the moments of linear combinations of
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
are not large for sufficiently large
t_{1}-t_{2},
and it suffices to study the products
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}
to bound the moments of the quadratic form
Q
in (
12
).

Proof of Theorem
14
.

1. The proof of equation (
13
)

We first prove (
13
).
Since
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
for
|t_{1}-t_{2}|<B
,

\displaystyle\left\|Q\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

Define
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,a}
and
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t,a}
as in (
A.1
). If
t_{2}>t_{1}
, then

\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]=\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]\right)=0.

Since
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}=\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1},
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right].

This further implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]\right).

Notice that

\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,0}\right]+\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,q-1}\right]\right)=\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,q-1}),

where
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t,-1}=0.
We have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2},

where
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right].

For any
q\geq 0
and any
s=1,2,\cdots,T-B
, define

\displaystyle A_{s,q}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-B-s+1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1},

and
\mathcal{A}_{s,q}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},\cdots,e_{T-B-s+1-q}
. Then
\mathcal{A}_{s,q}\subset\mathcal{A}_{s+1,q}
,
A_{s,q}
is measurable in
\mathcal{A}_{s,q}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(A_{s+1,q}-A_{s,q}\right)\mid\mathcal{A}_{s,q}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=T-s}^{T}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-T+B+s-1}\mid\mathcal{A}_{s,q}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=T-s}^{T}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-T+B+s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}\right)\mid\mathcal{A}_{s,q}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=T-s}^{T}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-T+B+s-1}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}\right)=0,

so
A_{s,q}
forms a martingale. According to Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.8)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

Since
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}
is independent of
e_{t_{2}+1},\cdots,e_{T},
we have

\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T,T-t_{1}-1}\right].

For any given vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d}
, from Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.9)

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T,T-t_{1}-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}.

Since
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}
is independent of
\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
, from Theorem 1.7 in
Shao, 2003
and (
A.9
), for any random vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\left|(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right|^{M/2}\right)\mid\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}=\boldsymbol{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right)^{M/4}\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|_{2}^{M/2}=C\left(\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right)^{M/4}\left|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right|_{2}^{M/2}.

This observation implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}.

From (
A.8
),

\displaystyle\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.10)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}.

For any
q=0,1,\cdots,
and any
s=1,2,\cdots,T-B
, define

\displaystyle D_{q,s}=\sum_{t_{2}=T-s+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{D}_{q,s}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-s+1-q}
. Then
D_{q,s}
is measurable in
\mathcal{D}_{q,s}
,
\mathcal{D}_{q,s}\subset\mathcal{D}_{q,s+1}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(D_{q,s+1}-D_{q,s}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-s-B}b_{t_{1}(T-s)}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,T-s-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,T-s-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-s-B}b_{t_{1}(T-s)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,T-s-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,T-s-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]\right)=0,

so
D_{q,s}
forms a martingale. According to Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.11)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

In the following step, we try to bound the norm

\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

If
q\leq B
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\left|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]\right\|_{M/2}+\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}.

From Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.12)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{Cd}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}.

Furthermore, since
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{Cd}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\sum_{s=B}^{\infty}\frac{Cd}{s^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha-1}}

for a constant
C_{1}.
From (
A.11
), if
q\leq B,
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.13)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

On the other hand, if
q>B,
we perform the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.14)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For the first term,
q\geq t_{2}-t_{1}
, and we rearrange
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
to

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}=\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}=\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}+\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}.

Since
q-1\geq t_{2}-t_{1}-1,

\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}.

From Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle=\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}+\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{C}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle=\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}+\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{C}{(1+t_{1}+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}.

Notice that
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,
so

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{1}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}\times(1+t_{1}+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}-q}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha-1}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}<q+1.\end{cases}

From Lemma
A.2
, and notice that
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha-1}}.

Therefore, if
q>B,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-q)}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.15)

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha-1}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha-1}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}<q+1.\end{cases}

Now we consider the second term in (
A.14
). If
t_{2}\leq q+1
, then
t_{2}-q-1<1
and the second term equals
0
. Otherwise, if

t_{2}\geq q+2
, for the second term, from Lemma
A.2
, and notice that
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]\right\|_{M/2}+\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-q-1}\frac{1}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{(1+q)^{\alpha-1}}.

Combine the aforementioned results, if
q>B
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha-1}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha-1}}\quad\text{if}\quad t_{2}<q+1.\end{cases}

From (
A.11
), if
q>B
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\left(\frac{d^{2}}{q^{2\alpha-2}}+\frac{d^{2}}{B^{2\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}\right)}+C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}\frac{d^{2}}{B^{2\alpha}}+\frac{d^{2}}{q^{2\alpha-2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}d\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1}+\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}}.

If
q\leq T-1,
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}=\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}=\sum_{s=2}^{q-B+1}\frac{1}{s^{2\alpha-2}}\leq C

and

\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1=T-q\leq T,

in such case

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.16)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}.

On the other hand, if
q\geq T,
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}=\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha-2}}=\sum_{s=2+q-T}^{q-B+1}\frac{1}{s^{2\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C}{(2+q-T)^{2\alpha-3}},

and

\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1=0.

In such case

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.17)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{(2+q-T)^{2\alpha-3}}}.

From (
A.13
), (
A.16
), and (
A.17
),

\displaystyle\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.18)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=0}^{B}\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha-1}}+\sum_{q=B+1}^{T-1}\left(\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)+\sum_{q=T}^{\infty}\left(\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{(2+q-T)^{2\alpha-3}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}d\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}dT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha}}.

From (
A.10
) and (
A.18
), and notice that
d\asymp T,
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\dagger}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2},

where
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\dagger}=b_{t_{2}t_{1}}
, the aforementioned results also hold and we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}.

This proves eq.(
13
).

2. The proof of equation (
14
).

We then prove equation (
14
). Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

Suppose
t_{2}>t_{1}.
In this case, notice that

\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}=\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell},

where
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}},
and is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}.
From this decomposition, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.19)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+\ell-t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{q=\ell+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For the first term in (
A.19
), notice that

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+\ell-t_{2}}\right]=\sum_{q=t_{1}+\ell-t_{2}+1}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1},\quad\text{and}\quad t_{2}\leq t_{1}+\ell.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+\ell-t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}\sum_{q=t_{1}+\ell-t_{2}+1}^{\infty}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For any given
q\geq 1
and
s=1,\cdots,T-B,
define

\displaystyle M_{q,s}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-B-s+1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})

and
\mathcal{M}_{q,s}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-B-s+1-q},
then
M_{q,s}
is measurable in
\mathcal{M}_{q,s}
,
\mathcal{M}_{q,s}\subset\mathcal{M}_{q,s+1}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(M_{q,s+1}-M_{q,s}\right)\mid\mathcal{M}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=(T-s)\vee(T-B-s+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(T-B-s+\ell)}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-T+B+s-1}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1})\mid\mathcal{M}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=(T-s)\vee(T-B-s+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(T-B-s+\ell)}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-T+B+s-1}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1})=0,

so
M_{q,s}
forms a martingale. According to Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

For any
t_{2}
such that
(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)\leq t_{2}\leq T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell),

\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T,T-t_{1}-1}\right].

From Lemma
A.2
, for any vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T,T-t_{1}-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T,T-t_{1}-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C|\boldsymbol{\tau}|_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}.

Since
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}
is independent of
\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1},
from Theorem 1.7 in
Shao, 2003
, for any vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\ (\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\ \right|^{M/2}\mid\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}=\boldsymbol{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\ \boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\ \right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{M/4}|\boldsymbol{\tau}|_{2}^{M/2}

\displaystyle=C\left(\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{M/4}|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}|_{2}^{M/2},

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.20)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}.

For the second term in (
A.19
), notice that
t_{2}\geq t_{1}+\ell+1,
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2},

where
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},-1}=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right]=0.
For any
q\geq 0
and any
s=1,2,\cdots,T-B,
define

\displaystyle G_{q,s}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-B-s+1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})

and
\mathcal{G}_{q,s}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},\cdots,e_{T-B-s+1-q}
. Then
G_{q,s}
is measurable in
\mathcal{G}_{q,s},

\mathcal{G}_{q,s}\subset\mathcal{G}_{q,s+1},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(G_{q,s+1}-G_{q,s}\right)\mid\mathcal{G}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=T-B-s+\ell+1}^{T}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}\right)\mid\mathcal{G}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=T-B-s+\ell+1}^{T}b_{(T-B-s)t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{T-B-s,q-1})=0,

so
G_{q,s}
forms a martingale.From Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\left\|(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

Since
t_{2}\geq t_{1}+\ell+1
in the summation, we have
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}
is independent of
\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}.
In addition, for any vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},
from (
A.7
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C|\boldsymbol{\tau}|_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}.

Therefore, from Theorem 1.7 of
Shao, 2003
, for any vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}\right|^{M/2}\mid\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}=\boldsymbol{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}\right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|_{2}^{M/2}\left(\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{M/4}=C\left|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right|_{2}^{M/2}\left(\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{M/4},

and

\displaystyle\left\|(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1})^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}^{(j)}\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}.

This result implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},\ell}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.21)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}\sum_{q=0}^{\infty}\delta_{q}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}.

For the third term in (
A.19
), notice that

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right).

For any given
q\geq\ell+1
and any
s=1,2,\cdots T-B,
define

\displaystyle U_{q,s}=\sum_{t_{2}=T-s+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{U}_{q,s}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-s+1-q}.
Then
U_{q,s}
is measurable in
\mathcal{U}_{q,s},

\mathcal{U}_{q,s}\subset\mathcal{U}_{q,s+1},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(U_{q,s+1}-U_{q,s}\right)\mid\mathcal{U}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-s-B}b_{t_{1}(T-s)}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,(T-s-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,(T-s-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{U}_{q,s}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-s-B}b_{t_{1}(T-s)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,(T-s-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{T-s,(T-s-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s,q-1}\right]\right)=0,

so
U_{q,s}
forms a martingale. Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
implies

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.22)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

Since
(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell\leq\ell
and
\ell+1\leq q
, we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}+\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}+(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}),

and
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

From Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.23)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left(\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{Cd}{q^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}|b_{t_{1},t_{2}}|\leq\frac{CdT}{q^{\alpha}}.

Also since
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\sum_{j=1}^{d}|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{(j)}\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M}.

Since
q\geq\ell+1,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\leq\sum_{s=((t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell)+1}^{q-1}\delta_{s}\leq\frac{C}{(1+(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell)^{\alpha}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}+q-t_{2}-1}\right]\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}},

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}^{(j)}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}}{(1+(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell)^{\alpha}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}}{(1+(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-\ell-1}\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}+\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-\ell)}^{t_{2}-B}\frac{Cd\times\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-\ell-1}\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}+\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-\ell)}^{t_{2}-B}\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}.

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-\ell-1}\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}\leq\begin{cases}C\quad\text{if }t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{C}{(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }t_{2}<q+1,\end{cases}\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{t_{1}=1\vee(t_{2}-\ell)}^{t_{2}-B}\delta_{t_{1}+q-t_{2}}\leq\frac{C}{(1+(1+q-t_{2})\vee(q-\ell))^{\alpha}},

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},q-1}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.24)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}\left(1+(1+q-t_{2})\vee(q-\ell)\right)^{\alpha}}+\begin{cases}\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }t_{2}<q+1.\end{cases}

From (
A.23
) and (
A.24
), and notice that

(1+q-t_{2})\vee(q-\ell)\geq q-\ell,\quad\text{and}\quad\ell+1\leq q\quad\text{for the third term,}

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CdT}{q^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\begin{cases}\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }t_{2}\geq q+1,\\
\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }t_{2}<q+1.\end{cases}

Furthermore, from (
A.22
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\left(\frac{dT}{q^{\alpha}}+\frac{d}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\frac{d}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}(2+q-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}\left(\frac{dT}{q^{\alpha}}+\frac{d}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\frac{d}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}dT\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}d\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha}}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1}.

If
q\leq T-1,
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha}}=\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha}}\leq C\quad\text{and }\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1=T-q.

If
q\geq T,
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{q\wedge T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha}}=\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\frac{1}{(2+q-t_{2})^{2\alpha}}\leq\frac{C}{(2+q-T)^{2\alpha-1}}\quad\text{and }\sum_{t_{2}=1+q}^{T}1=0.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\begin{cases}\frac{Cd(1+\sqrt{T-q})}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\quad\text{if }q\leq T-1,\\
\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(2+q-T)^{\alpha-1/2}}\quad\text{if }q\geq T.\end{cases}

From this observation, notice that
\ell\geq B,

\displaystyle\sum_{q=\ell+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},(t_{2}-t_{1}-1)\wedge\ell}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.25)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{q=\ell+1}^{\infty}\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{q^{\alpha}}+\sum_{q=\ell+1}^{\infty}\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}(1+q-\ell)^{\alpha}}+\sum_{q=\ell+1}^{T-1}\frac{Cd(1+\sqrt{T-q})}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}+\sum_{q=T}^{\infty}\frac{Cd}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(2+q-T)^{\alpha-1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}dT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}d\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}dT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}dT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{2}d\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}.

From (
A.20
), (
A.21
), and (
A.25
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.26)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}+C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B\vee(\ell+1-q)}^{\ell}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}+C\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{2}-t_{1}|\geq\ell+1}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}.

On the other hand, define
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\dagger}=b_{t_{2}t_{1}}
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\dagger}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}+B)\vee(t_{1}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{1}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\dagger 2}}+C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{\dagger 2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+B)\vee(t_{2}+\ell+1-q)}^{T\wedge(t_{2}+\ell)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+\ell+1}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B\vee(\ell+1-q)}^{\ell}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{2}}+C\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{2}-t_{1}|\geq\ell+1}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CdT\sqrt{T}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}.

These two inequalities prove (
14
).
∎

Remark 12
.

The proof of equation (
13
) decomposes the quadratic form
Q
into a series of martingales

\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}\right)^{\top}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1},\quad q\geq 0,

(A.27)

where
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1};
together with remainder terms of the form

\sum_{t_{2}=1+B}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},q-1}\right]\right),\quad q\geq 0.

(A.28)

From (
A.10
), the sum of squares of coefficients
\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T-B}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{T}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}
in (
13
) arises from (
A.27
). The independence between
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q}-\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{t_{1},q-1}
makes the moment of the summation in (
A.27
) behave similarly to those of independent random variables.

From (
A.18
), the terms
\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}
and
\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}
in (
13
) arise from (
A.28
). We note that the products
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
may exhibit complex dependence structures. In particular, if the time lag
a=t_{2}-t_{1}
is large, the dependence between
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{1}+a,a-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2}+a,a-1}
can remain strong even when

t_{1}-t_{2}
is large in absolute value.
Fortunately, after selecting a sufficiently large bandwidth
B,
Lemma
A.2
ensures that the moments of the elements of
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
remain small. Hence, when controlling the moments of the summations (
A.28
), it is unnecessary to analyze the dependence structure of

\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}
in detail.

Remark 13
.

In the context of ANOVA, our test statistic introduces two thresholds
B<B_{1},
and assumes that
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
whenever
|t_{1}-t_{2}|<B
or
|t_{1}-t_{2}|>B_{1}.
Under this assumption, by choosing
\ell>B_{1},
we have

\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|\geq\ell+1}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0,
and

\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B\vee(\ell+1-q)}^{\ell}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}=\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B\vee(\ell+1-q)}^{B_{1}}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
if
q<\ell+1-B_{1}.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=\ell+1-B_{1}}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B}^{B_{1}}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=B}^{B_{1}}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{(1+\ell)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}.

The proof of Theorem
4.3
leverages the technique used in the proof of Theorem 4 of
Xu et al., 2019
. Specifically, it introduces the function
g_{\psi,t}(\cdot)
in (
A.29
) as a continuously differentiable approximation to the indicator function.
This approximation allows statisticians to use the Taylor expansion to approximate the expectation of
g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)
and the expectation of
g_{\psi,x}
applied to a normal random variable. Theorem
14
also plays a decisive role in the proof of Theorem
4.3
, which enables the use of “
m
-approximation” technique—
approximating the original time series by a sequence of “
m
-dependent” random variables (see Definition 6.4.3 of
Brockwell and Davis, 1991
), which is also illustrated in Section C.1 of
Zhang and Wu, 2017
.
Theorem
4.3
is further employed to bound the moments of the blocks defined in (
A.34
) and (
B.13
).

Proof of Theorem
4.3
.

Define the functions

g_{0}(x)=\left(1-\min\left(1,\max\left(x,0\right)\right)^{4}\right)^{4}\quad\text{and}\quad g_{\psi,t}(x)=g_{0}\left(\psi\left(x-t\right)\right).

(A.29)

According to (S2) and (S4)-(S5) of
Xu et al., 2019
, there exists a constant
g_{*}
such that

\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime}(x)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi,\quad\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime\prime}(x)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi^{2},\quad\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime\prime\prime}(x)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi^{3},

and

\mathbf{1}_{x\leq t}\leq g_{\psi,t}(x)\leq\mathbf{1}_{x\leq t+1/\psi},

(A.30)

where
\mathbf{1}_{x\leq t}
denotes the indicator function, which equals
1
if
x\leq t,
and 0 otherwise.
From (
A.30
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\xi\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-\frac{1}{\psi}}(\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\xi)\right]\right|+\mathbf{Pr}\left(x-\frac{1}{\psi}\leq\xi\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\xi)\right]\right|+\frac{C}{\psi},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\xi\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-\frac{1}{\psi}}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-\frac{1}{\psi}}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-\frac{1}{\psi}}(\xi)\right]\right|-\mathbf{Pr}\left(x-\frac{1}{\psi}\leq\xi\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\xi)\right]\right|-\frac{C}{\psi}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{Pr}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\xi\leq x\right)\right|\leq\frac{C}{\psi}+\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\xi)\right]\right|.

(A.31)

For any integer
\ell>B_{1},
from (
14
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{Q}{S_{T}}-\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.32)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=\ell+1-B_{1}}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{|t_{1}-t_{2}|=\ell+1-q}^{B_{1}}b^{2}_{t_{1}t_{2}}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{S_{T}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{S_{T}B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{dT(B_{1}-B)}}{S_{T}(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{S_{T}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{S_{T}B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{1/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right)\right]\right|

(A.33)

\displaystyle\leq g_{*}\psi\left\|\frac{Q}{S_{T}}-\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\psi}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\psi T^{3/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C\psi T^{1/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

Notice that
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
for
|t_{1}-t_{2}|<B
or
|t_{1}-t_{2}|>B_{1},
so we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{2}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right),

where
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}=\left(b_{t_{1}t_{2}}+b_{t_{2}t_{1}}\right)/2.
With this definition
\left|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\right|\leq 1,
and
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}=0
if
\left|t_{1}-t_{2}\right|<B
or
\left|t_{1}-t_{2}\right|>B_{1}.
For an integer
v>\ell,
define the big-block

A_{q}=2\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right),

(A.34)

and the small-block

a_{q}=2\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right).

(A.35)

Define
R=\lceil\frac{T}{v+\ell}\rceil,
where
\lceil x\rceil
represents the smallest integer that is larger than or equal to
x.
Then

\displaystyle\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}+\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}

(A.36)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{q=1}^{R}\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right).

Furthermore,
A_{q}
are mutually independent, and
a_{q}
are mutually independent. From Theorem 2 of
Whittle, 1960
,

\displaystyle\left\|2\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{q=1}^{R}\left\|a_{q}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\left\|a_{q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ 2}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+C\left(\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\right)\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q(v+\ell))\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ 2}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\ell(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.37)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{S_{T}}\left(\sqrt{Rd\ell(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{\sqrt{R}T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{\sqrt{R}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{\sqrt{R}T^{5/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{1}T}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}},

and

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right)\right]\right.

(A.38)

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\psi}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\psi\left(\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{T}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{T^{2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}\right).

Define
A^{*}_{q},q=1,\cdots,R,
as independent normal random variables such that

\mathbf{E}\left[A^{*}_{q}\right]=0,\quad\mathrm{Var}(A^{*}_{q})=\mathrm{Var}(A_{q}),

and
A^{*}_{q_{1}}
and
A_{q_{2}}
are independent for different
q_{1}
and
q_{2}.
Define the summation

T_{u}=\sum_{q=1}^{u-1}A_{q}+\sum_{q=u+1}^{R}A^{*}_{q},\quad\text{then}\quad T_{u}+A_{u}=T_{u+1}+A^{*}_{u+1},

where
A_{u},A^{*}_{u}
are independent of
T_{u}.
From Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]

(A.39)

\displaystyle=g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)+g_{\psi,x}^{\prime}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\frac{A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right]+\frac{1}{2}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\frac{A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{1})\left(\frac{A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)^{3}\mid T_{u}\right]

\displaystyle-g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)-g_{\psi,x}^{\prime}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\frac{A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right]-\frac{1}{2}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{T_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mathbf{E}\left(\frac{A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{6}\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{2})\left(\frac{A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)^{3}\mid T_{u}\right],

where
\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2}
are two random variables. Since

\mathbf{E}\left[A_{u}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[A_{u}^{*}\right]=0,\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{u}\right)=\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{u}^{*}\right),

and
A_{u}^{*}
has normal distribution, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]\right|

(A.40)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{6}\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{1})\left(\frac{A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)^{3}\mid T_{u}\right]\right|+\left|\frac{1}{6}\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(\zeta_{2})\left(\frac{A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)^{3}\mid T_{u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\psi^{3}}{S^{3}_{T}}\left(\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}+\left\|A_{u}^{*}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}\right)\leq\frac{C_{1}\psi^{3}\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}}{S^{3}_{T}}.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right\|_{M/2}

(A.41)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}\right]-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right)\right\|_{M/2}
