\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{S_{T}}\left(\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ 2}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{S_{T}}\left(\left(\sum_{q=1}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\right)\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ 2}}+\frac{dT^{3/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{d\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{S_{T}}\left(\sqrt{dv(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{\frac{v}{T}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{\sum_{q=1}^{R}A^{*}_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]\right|

(A.42)

\displaystyle=\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{R}+A_{R}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{1}+A_{1}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=1}^{R}\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{T_{u}+A_{u}^{*}}{S_{T}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=1}^{R}\frac{C\psi^{3}\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}}{S^{3}_{T}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\psi^{3}R\left(\frac{v^{3/2}}{T^{3/2}}+\frac{T^{9/2}}{B^{3\alpha}(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{3\alpha-6}(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}+\frac{T^{9/2}}{\ell^{3\alpha-3}(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\psi^{3}\left(\sqrt{\frac{v}{T}}+\frac{T^{11/2}}{B^{3\alpha}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{3\alpha-6}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}+\frac{T^{11/2}}{\ell^{3\alpha-3}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}\right).

Finally, notice that
\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}^{*}
has normal distribution with mean
0
and variance

\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}^{*}\right)=\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}\mathrm{Var}\left(A^{*}_{q}\right)=\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}\mathrm{Var}(A_{q})=\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right\|_{2}^{2},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}^{*}\right)\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Z}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right\|_{2}\right)\right],

(A.43)

where
Z
is a normal random with standard normal distribution. Similarly, since
\xi
has normal distribution with
\mathbf{E}[\xi]=0
and
\mathrm{Var}(\xi)=\mathrm{Var}\left(Q/S_{T}\right),
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\xi\right)\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Z}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}\right)\right],

where
Z
is the standard normal random variable that coincides with that in (
A.43
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}^{*}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\xi\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Z}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right\|_{2}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{Z}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\psi}{S_{T}}\left|\ \left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}\ \right|

\displaystyle.\leq\frac{C\psi}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}-\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}.

From (
A.36
),

\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}+\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right),

so from (
A.32
) and (
A.37
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}-\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q}}{S_{T}}\right\|_{2}+\frac{1}{S_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{1/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\frac{1}{S_{T}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q}^{*}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\xi\right)\right]\right|

(A.44)

\displaystyle\leq C\psi\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{C\psi T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C\psi T}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C\psi T^{2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C\psi}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\psi T^{3/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C\psi T^{1/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}

We choose the parameters

\psi=\log(T),\quad\ell=\lfloor T^{\frac{3\kappa_{2}+1}{4}}\rfloor,\quad\text{and}\quad v=\lfloor T^{\frac{\kappa_{2}+1}{2}}\rfloor,

where
\lfloor x\rfloor
denotes the largest integer that is smaller than or equal to
x.

In such case

\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}\asymp T^{\frac{\kappa_{2}-1}{8}},\quad\sqrt{\frac{v}{T}}\asymp T^{\frac{\kappa_{2}-1}{4}},\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{1}{(1+\ell-B_{1})^{\alpha}}\asymp T^{-\frac{\alpha(1+3\kappa_{2})}{4}}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\frac{T^{3/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\ell^{\alpha-1}}=O\left(T^{\frac{7-\alpha}{4}-\frac{(3\alpha-1)\kappa_{2}}{4}}\right),\quad\frac{T^{1/2}}{\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}=O\left(T^{\frac{1-\kappa_{2}}{2}-\alpha\kappa_{1}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{T^{2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}=O\left(T^{\frac{8-\alpha}{4}-\frac{3\alpha\kappa_{2}}{4}}\right),\quad\frac{T}{B^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}=O\left(T^{\frac{3(1-\kappa_{2})}{4}-(\alpha-2)\kappa_{1}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{v(B_{1}-B)}}=O\left(T^{\frac{7-3\kappa_{2}}{4}-\alpha\kappa_{1}}\right),\quad\frac{T^{11/2}}{B^{3\alpha}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}=O\left(T^{5-2\kappa_{2}-3\alpha\kappa_{1}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{T^{11/2}}{\ell^{3\alpha-3}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}=O\left(T^{\frac{23-3\alpha}{4}-\frac{(9\alpha-1)\kappa_{2}}{4}}\right),\quad\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{3\alpha-6}v(B_{1}-B)^{3/2}}=O\left(T^{2-2\kappa_{2}-(3\alpha-6)\kappa_{1}}\right).

Since
\kappa_{1}>\frac{2}{\alpha}
and
\alpha>4,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1-\kappa_{2}}{2}-\alpha\kappa_{1}<\frac{1}{2}-2=-\frac{3}{2},\quad\frac{3(1-\kappa_{2})}{4}-(\alpha-2)\kappa_{1}<\frac{3}{4}-1=-\frac{1}{4},

\displaystyle\frac{7-3\kappa_{2}}{4}-\alpha\kappa_{1}<\frac{7}{4}-2=-\frac{1}{4},\quad 5-2\kappa_{2}-3\alpha\kappa_{1}<5-6=-1,

\displaystyle 2-2\kappa_{2}-(3\alpha-6)\kappa_{1}<2-3=-1.

On the other hand,
\frac{2}{\alpha}<\kappa_{2}<1,
so

\displaystyle 7-\alpha-(3\alpha-1)\kappa_{2}<1-\alpha+\kappa_{2}<-2,

\displaystyle 8-\alpha-3\alpha\kappa_{2}<2-\alpha<-2,

\displaystyle 23-3\alpha-(9\alpha-1)\kappa_{2}<5-3\alpha+\kappa_{2}<-6.

Therefore, from (
A.31
), (
A.33
), (
A.38
), (
A.42
), and (
A.44
), we prove (
15
).
∎

The proof of Theorem
4.5
relies on balancing the bias and variance of the variance estimator. However, compared to the setting of the classical Newey–West estimator, such as (5) in
Newey and West, 1987
, the variance of the quadratic form
Q
is influenced by fourth-order cumulants of time series data. Our work therefore weights products of the second-order residuals
\vartheta_{t}
defined in (
16
) instead of the original time series data to construct the variance estimator.

Another notable trick here is that our variance estimator does not incorporate bias arising from data covariances. Specifically, since
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
if
|t_{1}-t_{2}|<B
or
|t_{1}-t_{2}|>B_{1},
we have

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

(A.45)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=2}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-1}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)+\sum_{t_{2}=2}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-1}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right),

where
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}=(b_{t_{1}t_{2}}+b_{t_{2}t_{1}})/2.
The variance of
Q
is then given by

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)=\frac{4}{S_{T}^{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\right]={\Gamma}-{\Theta},

(A.46)

\displaystyle\text{where}\quad{\Gamma}=\frac{4}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}\sum_{t_{3}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{4}=1\vee(t_{3}-B_{1})}^{t_{3}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}b_{t_{3}t_{4}}^{\circ}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{3}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{4}}\right],

\displaystyle\text{and}\quad{\Theta}=\frac{4}{S_{T}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}\sum_{t_{3}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{4}=1\vee(t_{3}-B_{1})}^{t_{3}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}b_{t_{3}t_{4}}^{\circ}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{3}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{4}}\right].

The following proof establishes that the proposed estimator is consistent for
\Gamma,
and that
\Theta
is negligible compared to
\Gamma.
This phenomenon arises because
b_{t_{1}t_{2}}=0
if
|t_{1}-t_{2}|<B,
and is crucial in our setup—the time series data are not assumed to be stationary, implying that
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]
can vary with respect to different time lag
|t_{1}-t_{2}|.
Consequently, estimating each individual covariance term
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]
would in general be infeasible without imposing further structural assumptions. Fortunately, since
\Theta
is negligible compared to
\Gamma,
estimating
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]
becomes unnecessary.

Proof of Theorem
4.5
.

The proof is separated into two parts: that
\Theta
is small and that
\Gamma
can be estimated from our estimator. From (
A.12
) and use the same notation as in (
A.1
), for any
t_{2}>t_{1},

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{t_{2},t_{2}-t_{1}-1}\right]\right|\leq\frac{Cd}{(t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}.

Notice that
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|\leq 1,
so
|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}|\leq\left(|b_{t_{1}t_{2}}|+|b_{t_{2}t_{1}}|\right)/2\leq 1,
and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}b_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{\circ}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}\frac{1}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}d\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\frac{1}{B^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\left|\Theta\right|\leq\frac{C}{S^{2}_{T}}\frac{T^{4}}{B^{2\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}T^{4}}{Td(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}.

(A.47)

From the assumptions in Theorem
4.5
,

\displaystyle\frac{T^{2}}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}=O\left(T^{2-(2\alpha-2)\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}\right)=O(1/T).

Therefore, the bias
\left|\Theta\right|
is negligible compared to
\Gamma,
so its estimation is unnecessary.

We then prove the consistency of the estimation of
\Gamma.
From (
16
),

\displaystyle\Gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{3}=B+1}^{T}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t_{2}=1\vee(t_{1}-B_{1})}^{t_{1}-B}b^{\circ}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right)\left(\sum_{t_{4}=1\vee(t_{3}-B_{1})}^{t_{3}-B}b^{\circ}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{3}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{4}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{3}=B+1}^{T}\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{3}}\right],

so

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\Gamma\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|.

Define

\displaystyle\iota_{t}=\vartheta_{t}-\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t}\right]=2\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}b^{\circ}_{tt_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right),

then
\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t}\right]=0,
and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\iota_{t_{1}}\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\iota_{t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\right]\right|,

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

(A.48)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right)\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\right]\right|\times\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\left\|\iota_{t}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}b^{\circ}_{tt_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}b^{\circ 2}_{tt_{2}}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}.

Since
B\asymp T^{\kappa_{1}},

\alpha\kappa_{1}>2,

\kappa_{1}<1/6,
and
\alpha>14,
we have

\left\|\iota_{t}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}.

(A.49)

On the other hand, from (
A.3
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}|b_{tt_{2}}^{\circ}|\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

(A.50)

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\frac{1}{(t-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

For any
a\geq B_{1},
from (
14
),

\displaystyle\left\|\iota_{t}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,a}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}b^{\circ}_{tt_{2}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,a}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(A.51)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{q=a+1-B_{1}}^{\infty}\delta_{q}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}b^{\circ 2}_{tt_{2}}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{a^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(a+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{a^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S_{T}^{2}}\sum_{q=0}^{T-B-1}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=0}^{B_{1}}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=B_{1}+1}^{H}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\left(\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=H+1}^{T-B-1}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\left(\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|.

From Definition
4.4
, we have
1-\mathcal{K}(x)=\mathcal{K}(0)-\mathcal{K}(x)\leq Cx
for any
x\in[0,1],
so from (
A.49
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=0}^{B_{1}}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CTd(B_{1}-B)}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{q=0}^{B_{1}}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=C\sum_{q=0}^{B_{1}}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\leq\sum_{q=0}^{B_{1}}\frac{C_{1}q}{H}\leq\frac{C_{2}B^{2}_{1}}{H}.

Similarly, from (
A.51
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=B_{1}+1}^{H}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\left(\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{q=B_{1}+1}^{H}\frac{q}{H}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{H}\sum_{q=B_{1}+1}^{H}\left(\frac{q}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{qT^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{qT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{1}}{H}\sum_{q=B_{1}+1}^{H}\left(\frac{1}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{B_{1}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{qT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}B_{1}}{H}+\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{HB_{1}^{\alpha-3}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{2}TH}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=H+1}^{T-B-1}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\left(\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{q=H+1}^{T-B-1}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}+q}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sum_{q=H+1}^{T-B-1}\left(\frac{1}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}}{(H-B_{1})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{H^{\alpha-2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}.

Therefore, notice that
B_{1}\asymp T^{\kappa_{2}}
with
\kappa_{1}<\kappa_{2}<1/6
and
H\asymp T^{1/3}\log(T),
we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

(A.52)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB^{2}_{1}}{H}+\frac{CT^{2}}{HB^{\alpha-3}_{1}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\frac{1}{3}-2\kappa_{2}}\log(T)}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/3}}{\log(T)T^{\kappa_{2}(\alpha-\frac{5}{2})}}+\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}.

From (
A.50
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(1-\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right)\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\right]\right|\times\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S_{T}^{2}}\left(\sum_{t=B+1}^{T}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t}\right]\right|\right)^{2}\leq\frac{C}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\frac{T^{2}d^{2}}{B^{2\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{CTd}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}.

Since
B\asymp T^{\kappa_{1}}
with
\frac{2}{\alpha}<\kappa_{1}<\frac{1}{6}
and
d\asymp T,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{Td}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{CT^{2}B^{2}}{(B_{1}-B)T^{4}}\leq C_{1}T^{-5/3}.

Therefore, from (
A.48
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\left(\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)-1\right)\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

(A.53)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T^{\frac{1}{3}-2\kappa_{2}}\log(T)}+\frac{CT^{5/3}}{\log(T)T^{\kappa_{2}(\alpha-\frac{5}{2})}}+\frac{C}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}+\frac{CTd}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\frac{1}{3}-2\kappa_{2}}\log(T)}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/3}}{\log(T)T^{\kappa_{2}(\alpha-\frac{5}{2})}}+\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{q=0}^{T-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}.

For any
t
and
s
, define

\omega_{t,s}=\begin{cases}\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,s}\right]\ \text{if }s\geq 0,\\
0\ \text{if }s<0,\end{cases}\quad\text{and}\quad\varphi_{t,s}=\iota_{t}-\omega_{t,s}.

(A.54)

From this definition,
\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}
is independent of
\iota_{t_{1}},
and

\mathbf{E}\left[\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\iota_{t_{1}}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\right]=0,\quad\text{therefore}\quad\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right].

Define

\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right].

For any
t_{1},

\displaystyle\iota_{t_{1}}=\sum_{q=0}^{T}\left(\omega_{t_{1},q}-\omega_{t_{1},q-1}\right)+\iota_{t_{1}}-\omega_{t_{1},T}=\sum_{q=0}^{T}(\omega_{t_{1},q}-\omega_{t_{1},q-1})+\varphi_{t_{1},T},

and

\displaystyle\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=0}^{T}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,q-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,T}\right]\right).

Therefore, for any
q=0,1,\cdots,T-B-1,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.55)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\iota_{t_{1}}\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\varphi_{t_{1},T}\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,T}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}.

For any given
l=0,1,\cdots,T,q=0,1,\cdots,T-B-1,
and
z=1,\cdots,T-q-B,
define

\displaystyle T_{q,l,z}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-q-z+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1},

and
\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-q-z+1-l},
then

\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z}\subset\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z+1},

T_{q,l,z}
is measurable in
\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(T_{q,l,z+1}-T_{q,l,z}\right)\mid\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[(\omega_{T-q-z,l}-\omega_{T-q-z,l-1})\omega_{T-z,q-1}\mid\mathcal{T}_{q,l,z}\right]

\displaystyle=\omega_{T-z,q-1}\left(\omega_{T-q-z,l-1}-\omega_{T-q-z,l-1}\right)=0,

so
T_{q,l,z}
forms a martingale. From Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}

(A.56)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/4}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

If
l\leq B_{1},
then

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)},

making

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}\leq C\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B).

If
l\geq B_{1}+1,
from (
A.51
),

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l}-\varphi_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}},

making

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{7/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}{B^{\alpha}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}

(A.57)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{B_{1}}\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{7/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{5/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{S^{2}_{T}}B_{1}\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)+\frac{C}{S^{2}_{T}}\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{S^{2}_{T}}T^{7/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{T}\frac{1}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{7/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}{S^{2}_{T}B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{B_{1}^{\alpha-3/2}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}.

Since
B_{1}\asymp T^{\kappa_{2}}
with
\kappa_{2}>\frac{4}{2\alpha-3},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{T^{3/2}}{B_{1}^{\alpha-3/2}}\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}

for a constant
C.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}(\omega_{t_{1},l}-\omega_{t_{1},l-1})\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(A.58)

Besides,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\varphi_{t_{1},T}\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/4}

(A.59)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},T}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(T-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{(T-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{1}T}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}.

For any given
l=0,1,\cdots,T
any
q=0,1,\cdots,T-B-1,
and any
z=1,2,\cdots,T-q-B,
define

\displaystyle N_{q,l,z}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-q-z+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-z+1-l},
then
N_{q,l,z}
is measurable in
\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z},

\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z}\subset\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z+1},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[N_{q,l,z+1}-N_{q,l,z}\mid\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{T-q-z}\varphi_{T-z,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{T-q-z}\varphi_{T-z,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,l-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{N}_{q,l,z}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{T-q-z}\varphi_{T-z,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,l-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{T-q-z}\varphi_{T-z,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,l-1}\right]=0,

so
N_{q,l,z}
forms a martingale, and from Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al., 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M/4}}.

If
l\leq q,
then

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]\right\|_{M/4}+\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2},

making

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

Otherwise if
l\geq q+1,
we notice that

\displaystyle\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}

\displaystyle=\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}

\displaystyle=\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}+\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}=\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}+\left(\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right).

Since
q\leq l-1,

\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1},
and

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},l-q}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},l-1-q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-q}-\omega_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2},

making

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-q}-\omega_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

If
l\geq q+1+B_{1},
then

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-q}-\omega_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l-q}-\varphi_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l-q}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}.

Otherwise if
l\leq q+B_{1},

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-q}-\omega_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{S^{2}_{T}}\left(\sum_{l=0}^{q}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}+\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}\left\|\omega_{t_{1},l-q}-\omega_{t_{1},l-q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\sum_{l=0}^{q}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}+\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{1}}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{(q+B_{1})\wedge T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{1}}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{l=q+B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

If
q\leq B_{1},
then

\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)},

notice that
\kappa_{2}>\frac{4}{2\alpha-3}
and
\kappa_{1}>2/\alpha,
so

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\sum_{l=0}^{q}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}+\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=0}^{q}\sqrt{T\left(\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\right)^{2}}+\frac{C}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{B_{1}}\sqrt{T\left(\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{T}\sqrt{T\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{T}\frac{1}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=B_{1}+1}^{T}\frac{1}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{2}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B_{1}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\leq\frac{C_{3}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}.

If
q\geq B_{1}+1,
then

\displaystyle\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\sum_{l=0}^{q}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}+\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cq}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cq}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{2}q}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CqT}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(q+1-B_{1})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}q^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}-\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|_{M/2},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}.

If
q\leq B_{1},
then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{(q+B_{1})\wedge T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{l=q+B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B_{1}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

If
q\geq B_{1}+1,
then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\sum_{l=q+1}^{(q+B_{1})\wedge T}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CB_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CB_{1}T}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

Since
\kappa_{2}>\frac{4}{2\alpha-3},
we also have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{l=q+B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,l-1}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}d(B_{1}-B)}\left(\sum_{l=q+B_{1}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(l-q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(l-q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C}{\sqrt{Td(B_{1}-B)}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{d(B_{1}-B)(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT}{dB^{\alpha}(B_{1}-B)}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B_{1}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

We remark that the last inequality holds because

\frac{T^{5/2}}{B_{1}^{\alpha-2}}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}

for a constant
C.

From these observations, if
q\leq B_{1},
for
\kappa_{2}>\frac{4}{2\alpha-3},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.60)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B_{1}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}.

On the other hand, if
B_{1}+1\leq q\leq T-B-1,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{l=0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,l-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.61)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CB_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha}}.

Finally, since

\displaystyle\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}=\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}=\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}+\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1},

and
\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,T}.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,T}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,T}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right)\left(\iota_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},T-q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}\right\|_{M/4}+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\left(\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right)\left(\iota_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},T-q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}\right\|_{M/2}+\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},T-q}\right\|_{M/2}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1}+q,T}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(T+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{C}{(T+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

If
q\leq T/2,
then
T-q\geq T/2>B_{1}
for sufficiently large
T,
and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},T-q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(T-q+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(T-q)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

Otherwise if
q>T/2,
then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left\|\omega_{t_{1}+q,T}-\omega_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\varphi_{t_{1},T-q}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C}{(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}T^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

Therefore, we have for any
q,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\varphi_{t_{1}+q,q-1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+q,T}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.62)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{(T+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{T^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{5/2}}{T^{\alpha-1}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}}.

If
q\leq B_{1},
from (
A.58
), (
A.59
), (
A.60
), (
A.62
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}.

(A.63)

On the other hand, if
B_{1}+1\leq q,
from (
A.58
), (
A.59
), (
A.61
), (
A.62
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T-q}\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{1}+q}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.64)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(q-B_{1})^{\alpha-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CT^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{CB_{1}T^{2}}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}(q-1)^{\alpha-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\iota_{t_{1}}\iota_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.65)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{q=0}^{T-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{q}{H}\right)\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}H}{\sqrt{T}}.

From (
A.49
) and (
A.50
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S_{T}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\iota_{t_{1}}\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S_{T}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left\|\iota_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{Td(B_{1}-B)}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}\frac{d}{B^{\alpha-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}TH}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha-1}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\iota_{t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left\|\iota_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CTH}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha-1}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\left(\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.66)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}H}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{CTH}{\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}B^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{CB_{1}H}{\sqrt{T}}.

From (
A.46
), (
A.47
), (
A.53
), and (
A.66
), since
\kappa_{2}<1/6
and
\frac{1}{6}>\kappa_{1}>\frac{2}{\alpha},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.67)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\Gamma\right\|_{M/4}+\left|\Theta\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB_{1}H}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C}{T^{\frac{1}{3}-2\kappa_{2}}\log(T)}+\frac{CT^{5/3}}{\log(T)T^{\kappa_{2}(\alpha-\frac{5}{2})}}+\frac{C}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{CT^{2}}{(B_{1}-B)B^{2\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}H}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C}{T^{\frac{1}{3}-2\kappa_{2}}\log(T)}+\frac{C}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}.

Since

\displaystyle\frac{HB_{1}}{\sqrt{T}}=O\left(\frac{\log(T)}{T^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}\right),

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{S^{2}_{T}}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\vartheta_{t_{1}}\vartheta_{t_{2}}-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{Q}{S_{T}}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(A.68)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log(T)}{T^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{1}{T^{\kappa_{2}/2}}\right),

which proves (
17
)
∎

Appendix B
Proofs of theoretical results in Section
5

In the proofs of the results in Section
5
, we introduce additional notation for the filters: For any
k=1,2,\cdots,K,

t\in\mathbf{Z},
and
\ell=0,1,2,\cdots,
define
\mathcal{F}_{t,\ell}^{(k)}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{t,k},e_{t-1,k},\cdots,e_{t-\ell,k}.
This new notation is introduced to reflect the fact that the data in this section are generated from
K
different populations.

The proof of Theorem
21
leverages Theorem
14
to establish both the consistency and the asymptotic distributional results for the test statistics
\widehat{R}.

Proof of Theorem
21
.

1. The proof of equation (
19
).
From (
5
),

\displaystyle\widehat{R}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}\right|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{k=2}^{K}\left(\widehat{R}_{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\right),

and

\displaystyle\widehat{R}_{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}\right|_{2}^{2}

(B.1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}+\frac{2}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}+\frac{2}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{T_{k}T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\frac{2}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\frac{2}{T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

(B.2)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|.

From Theorem
14
, for any
k=1,\cdots,K,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(B.3)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left(\sqrt{d\times\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}1^{2}}+\frac{T^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{V_{k}}}\right).

From (
6
),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{(j)}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}^{(j)}\right]\right|

(B.4)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\frac{1}{(1+|t_{1}-t_{2}|)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}dT_{k}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}B^{\alpha-1}}=O\left(\frac{B}{B_{1}\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}}\right).

From Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}\sum_{B\leq|t_{2}-t_{1}|\leq B_{1}}1\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}\right|_{2}^{2}\times\left(\sum_{B\leq|t_{2}-t_{1}|\leq B_{1}}1\right)^{2}}.

For any given
t_{1}=1,2,\cdots,T_{k},

\displaystyle\sum_{B\leq|t_{2}-t_{1}|\leq B_{1}}1=\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1\asymp(B_{1}-B),

so

\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}|_{2}\sqrt{T_{k}}\times(B_{1}-B)}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}=O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}\right).

(B.5)

From Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}\right|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}\right\|_{M}\leq\frac{C}{T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{1}}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}\right|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}.

Define
\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,1},
then

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T_{1}T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right\|_{M/2}=\left\|\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}.

Since
\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}
for
k\geq 2,
from Lemma
A.2
and Theorem 1.7 in
Shao, 2003
, for any vector
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right|^{M/2}\mid\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}=\boldsymbol{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\tau}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|_{2}^{M/2}}{\left(\sqrt{T_{k}d}\right)^{M/2}}=\frac{C\left|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}\right|_{2}^{M/2}}{\left(\sqrt{T_{k}d}\right)^{M/2}},

so

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\left\|\ |\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}}{\sqrt{T_{k}d}}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T_{k}d}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}.

From Lemma
A.2
,

\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}=\frac{1}{T_{1}}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{(j)}_{t,1}\right\|\leq\frac{C}{T_{1}}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T_{1}}1^{2}}=\frac{C}{\sqrt{T_{1}}},

so

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T_{1}T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T_{1}T_{k}}}=O\left(\frac{1}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}\right).

(B.6)

From (
B.3
), (
B.4
), (
B.5
), and (
B.6
), we have

\left\|\ \widehat{R}_{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}\right|_{2}^{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}},

(B.7)

which proves (
19
).

2. The proof of equation (

).
From (
A.31
),

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{Pr}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\zeta\leq x\right)\right|

(B.8)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\psi}+\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\zeta)\right]\right|.

Under
H_{0},
we have
\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}=\cdots=\boldsymbol{\mu}_{K}.
From (
B.1
), for any
k=2,3,\cdots,K,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{R}_{k}-\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|+\frac{1}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}^{\top}_{t_{1},1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+2\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\frac{1}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}-\frac{1}{T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{T_{k}T_{1}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T_{1}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right\|_{M/2}.

For any
k=1,2,\cdots,K,
from Lemma
A.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\left(\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\left(\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}\right)^{2}}.

If
1+B_{1}\leq t_{1}\leq T_{k}-B_{1},
then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{t_{1}+B_{1}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}-B_{1}}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2(B_{1}-B+1)}{(2T_{k}-B-B_{1})\times(B_{1}-B+1)}-\frac{1}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{B+B_{1}}{T_{k}\times(2T_{k}-B-B_{1})}\asymp\frac{B_{1}}{T^{2}_{k}}.

If
t_{1}\leq B_{1}
or
t_{1}\geq T_{k}-B_{1}+1,
then for sufficiently large
\mathcal{T}_{\circ},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{t_{1}+B_{1}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}-B_{1}}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle=\frac{B+B_{1}}{T_{k}\times(2T_{k}-B-B_{1})}\leq\frac{2B_{1}}{T^{2}_{k}},

\displaystyle\text{and }\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}\geq-\frac{1}{T_{k}}.

Therefore, for sufficiently large
\mathcal{T}_{\circ},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}\right|\leq\frac{2B_{1}}{T^{2}_{k}}\vee\frac{1}{T_{k}}=\frac{1}{T_{k}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\left(\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B}^{(t_{1}+B_{1})\wedge T_{k}}1+\frac{1}{V_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}1-\frac{1}{T_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{B_{1}}\frac{1}{T^{2}_{k}}+\sum_{t_{1}=B_{1}+1}^{T_{k}-B_{1}}\frac{CB^{2}_{1}}{T^{4}_{k}}+\sum_{t_{1}=T_{k}-B_{1}+1}^{T_{k}}\frac{1}{T^{2}_{k}}\leq\frac{C_{1}B_{1}}{T^{2}_{k}},

which implies

\left\|\frac{1}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\frac{1}{T_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{B_{1}}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}.

(B.9)

From (
B.4
), (
B.6
), and (
B.9
),
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}_{k}-\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B_{1}}B}{B_{1}\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}}+\frac{C\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B_{1}}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}+\frac{CB_{1}\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}=O\left(\frac{B_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}\right).

For
\widehat{R}=\sum_{k=2}^{K}\widehat{R}_{k},
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\right)\right]\right.

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.+\left(K-1\right)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right)\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\psi\left\|\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\right.

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\left.-(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\psi B_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}},

making

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\right)\right]\right.

(B.10)

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\psi B_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}\right).

Choose integer

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}
