Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.\right.

(B.12)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.+\left(K-1\right)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right)\right]\right.

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left.\left.\left(K-1\right)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(1)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\psi\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+C\psi(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(1)}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\psi}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}\right).

Choose
v=\lfloor\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\frac{7}{12}}\rfloor,
and choose the big-blocks

\displaystyle A_{q,k}=2\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right),

the small-blocks

\displaystyle a_{q,k}=2\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q\times(v+\ell))\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)

for
q=1,2,\cdots,R=\lceil\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\dagger}}{v+\ell}\rceil.
Then

\displaystyle\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-B}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q,k},

and
A_{q,k}
are mutually independent with respect to different
q
or
k
; and
a_{q,k}
are mutually independent with respect to different
q
or
k.
Since
a_{q,k}
are mutually independent, from Theorem 2 of
Whittle, 1960
,

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\sum_{q=1}^{R}\left\|a_{q,k}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

From Theorem
14
, for any
q,k,

\displaystyle\left\|a_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q\times(v+\ell))\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q\times(v+\ell))\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}^{(k)}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q\times(v+\ell))\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}1^{2}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v+1}^{(q\times(v+\ell))\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}1^{2}}}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\ell\left(B_{1}-B\right)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

(B.13)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)\sqrt{d}}\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\dagger}}{v}}\sqrt{d\ell(B_{1}-B)}=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}\right),

and we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(k)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.\right.

(B.14)

\displaystyle+\left.\left.\left(K-1\right)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{2},\ell}^{(1)}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\psi\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}\right).

Define
A^{*}_{q,k},q=1,\cdots,R,k=1,\cdots,K,
as independent normal random variables such that

\mathbf{E}\left[A^{*}_{q,k}\right]=0,\quad\mathrm{Var}(A^{*}_{q,k})=\mathrm{Var}(A_{q,k}),

and
A^{*}_{q_{1},k_{1}}
and
A_{q_{2},k_{2}}
are independent for any
q_{1},q_{2},k_{1},k_{2}
. Define the summation

\displaystyle T_{u}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=1}^{u-1}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}A_{q,1}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{q=u+1}^{R}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}A_{q,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}A_{q,1}^{*}\right)

and

\displaystyle R_{u}=\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}A_{u,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}A_{u,1},

\displaystyle R^{*}_{u}=\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}A_{u,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}A_{u,1}^{*},

then
R^{*}_{u},R_{u}
are independent of
T_{u},
and

T_{u}+R_{u}=T_{u+1}+R^{*}_{u+1}.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\left\|A_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},\ell}^{(k)}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}1^{2}}+\frac{CT^{5/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{(\ell+1-B_{1})^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=(q-1)\times(v+\ell)+1}^{((q-1)\times(v+\ell)+v)\wedge T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B_{1})\vee 1}^{(t_{1}-B)}1^{2}}+\frac{CdT^{3/2}}{\ell^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cd\sqrt{T}}{B^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{dv(B_{1}-B)}.

Since
A^{*}_{q,k}
is normal random variable, we have

\displaystyle\left\|A_{q,k}^{*}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\left\|A_{q,k}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{dv(B_{1}-B)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|R_{u}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|A_{u,k}\right\|_{M/2}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\left\|A_{u,1}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\frac{v}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}},

and

\displaystyle\|R^{*}_{u}\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\|A_{u,k}^{*}\|_{M/2}+\frac{(K-1)\times\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\|A_{u,1}^{*}\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\frac{v}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}.

From (
A.39
),

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(T_{u}+R_{u}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(T_{u}+R^{*}_{u}\right)\mid T_{u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\psi^{3}\left(\left\|R_{u}\right\|^{3}_{M/2}+\left\|R_{u}^{*}\right\|^{3}_{M/2}\right)\leq C_{1}\psi^{3}\left(\frac{v}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}\right)^{3/2},

and

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right)\right]\right.

(B.15)

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}^{*}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=1}^{R}\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(T_{u}+R_{u})\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(T_{u}+R_{u}^{*})\right]\right|\leq C\psi^{3}\sqrt{\frac{v}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}.

Finally, the term

\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}^{*}

has normal distribution with mean
0
and variance

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}^{*}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=2}^{K}\sum_{q=1}^{R}\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{q,k}\right)+(K-1)^{2}\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{1}d}\sum_{q=1}^{R}\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{q,1}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right\|^{2}_{2}.

Since

\displaystyle\left|\ \left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right\|_{2}\right.

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\ \sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right\|_{M/2},

and for any
k=1,\cdots,K,
from (
B.11
) and (
B.13
),

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\ \sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)-\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{q=1}^{R}a_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}+\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}\vee t_{t},\ell}^{(k)}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{C}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}},

making

\displaystyle\left|\ \left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right\|_{2}\right.

(B.16)

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}\right).

Define
Z
as a standard normal random variable (normal random variable with mean
0
and variance
1
), then

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}^{*}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}^{*}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\zeta)\right]\right|

(B.17)

\displaystyle=\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,k}+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{q=1}^{R}A_{q,1}\right\|_{2}Z\right)\right]\right.

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\left\|\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.\left.+(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right\|_{2}Z\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\psi\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\frac{\psi}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}\right)

Choose
\psi=\log^{3}(T).
From (
B.8
), (
B.10
), (
B.12
), (
B.14
), (
B.15
), (
B.17
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{Pr}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\zeta\leq x\right)\right|

(B.18)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{\psi}+\frac{\psi B_{1}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}+\frac{\psi}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}}+\psi\sqrt{\frac{\ell}{v}}+\psi^{3}\sqrt{\frac{v}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}\right)=o(1),

which proves (
20
).
∎

Corollary B.1

(Further discussion of Remark
9
)
.

With any given threshold
\mathcal{\gamma}>0,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\leq\mathcal{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Pr}\left(\widehat{R}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\right).

We notice that
d\asymp\mathcal{T}_{\circ},
if the condition

\displaystyle 1=o\left(\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\right)

holds true, then for sufficiently large
\mathcal{T}_{\circ},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}<0,

and we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}\widehat{R}\leq\mathcal{\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{Pr}\left(\left|\ \widehat{R}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\ \right|\geq\left|\ \frac{\mathcal{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\ \right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbf{E}\left|\ \widehat{R}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\ \right|^{M/2}}{\left|\ \frac{\mathcal{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}\ \right|^{M/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}\times\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{K}|\boldsymbol{\mu}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{1}|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\mathcal{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}\right)\right)^{M/2}},

which tends to 0 as
\mathcal{T}_{\circ}
tends to infinity, making the power of the test tending to 1.

Since the normal distribution has a bounded density, the validity of Algorithm
1
depends on the consistent estimation of the variance of
\widehat{R},
as established in the following proof.

Proof of Theorem
5.2
.

Define the conditional mean and variance as follows:

\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\cdot\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\cdot\mid\mathbf{x}_{t,k},k=1,\cdots,K,t=1,\cdots,T_{k}\right],\quad\text{and}\quad\mathrm{Var}^{*}(x)=\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[x^{2}\right]-(\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[x\right])^{2}.

We also call such mean and variance “the expectation and the variance in the bootstrap world.” From Algorithm
1
,
\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}
has normal distribution in the bootstrap world, with mean

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,k}\mathbf{E}\left[\varepsilon^{*}_{t,k}\right]

\displaystyle+2(K-1)\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{t=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,1}\mathbf{E}\left[\varepsilon^{*}_{t,1}\right]=0,

and variance

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)

\displaystyle=4\sum_{k=2}^{K}\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V_{k}^{2}d}\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\sum_{t=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,k}\varepsilon^{*}_{t,k}\right)

\displaystyle+4(K-1)^{2}\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V_{1}^{2}d}\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\sum_{t=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,1}\varepsilon^{*}_{t,1}\right).

For any
k=1,2,\cdots,K,
from Step 4 of Algorithm
1
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\sum_{t=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,k}\varepsilon^{*}_{t,k}\right)=\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{2},k}\right)\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\left\|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{2},k}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right)\right)\right\|_{M/4},

where we define

\vartheta_{t,k}=\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}.

From Theorem
4.5
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{2},k}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\kappa_{2}/2}}\right).

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{2},k}\right)\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right)\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}.

From (
A.49
) and (
A.50
),

\displaystyle\left\|\vartheta_{t,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}+\left|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

(B.19)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{Cd}{B^{\alpha-1}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}.

Define

\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}=\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k},

then

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,k}-\vartheta_{t,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\left(\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}-\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right)-\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}+(B_{1}-B+1)\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+(B_{1}-B+1)\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{2}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}1^{2}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}.

From (
6
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t_{2}-B-1}\frac{1}{(1+t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}(B_{1}-B)}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

For any
p<q
and
k=1,\cdots,K,
notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

Since
\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k},
from Definition
4.1
and Theorem 1.7 of
Shao, 2003
, for any
\boldsymbol{\tau}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right|^{M/2}\mid\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]=\boldsymbol{\tau}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\boldsymbol{\tau}\right|_{2}^{M/2}

\displaystyle=C\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right|_{2}^{M/2},

making

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{(j)}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}^{(j)}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{(j)}\right\|_{M}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}^{(j)}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}^{(j)}\mid\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{q,q-p-1}\right]\right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{(q-p)^{\alpha}}.

If
p=q,
then

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{(j)}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{(j)}\right\|_{M/2}\leq Cd.

Therefore, for any
p,q,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{p,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{q,k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d}+\frac{Cd}{(1+|q-p|)^{\alpha}},

(B.20)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{2}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{2}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB(B_{1}-B)\sqrt{d}}{T_{k}}

\displaystyle+\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{2}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\frac{1}{(1+|t_{1}-t_{2}|)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\left(B_{1}-B\right).

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}1^{2}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}.

From (
6
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd(B_{1}-B)}{T_{k}B^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{1}(B_{1}-B)}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

From these observations, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\sum_{t_{2}=(t-B_{1})\vee 1}^{t-B}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{(B_{1}-B)}+\frac{CB_{1}}{B^{\alpha-1}}+C(B_{1}-B)\leq C_{1}B_{1}.

(B.21)

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t-B)\vee 1}^{(t+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right|.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}1^{2}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1},

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}1^{2}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{3/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}.

From (
6
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right|+\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{t-B-1}\frac{1}{(1+t-t_{1})^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=t+B+1}^{T_{k}}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}-t)^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{T_{k}B^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{2}}{B^{\alpha-1}}.

From (
B.20
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t-B)\vee 1}^{(t+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-B)\vee 1}^{(t+B)\wedge T_{k}}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CB\sqrt{d}}{T_{k}}+\frac{Cd}{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=t-B}^{t+B}\frac{1}{(1+|t_{1}-t|)^{\alpha}}\leq C_{1}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C.

(B.22)

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}_{k}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{1}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|.

From Theorem
14
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T^{2}_{k}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}1^{2}}+\frac{C}{T^{2}_{k}}\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}1^{2}}+\frac{CT_{k}^{1/2}}{B^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{T_{k}}B^{\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T_{k}}.

From (
6
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|+\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cd}{T^{2}_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{t_{1}-B-1}\frac{1}{(1+t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cd}{T^{2}_{k}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=t_{1}+B+1}^{T_{k}}\frac{1}{(1+t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}d}{T_{k}B^{\alpha-1}}.

From (
B.20
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{1}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{1}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\left\|\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T_{k}^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-B)\vee 1}^{(t_{1}+B)\wedge T_{k}}\left(C\sqrt{d}+\frac{Cd}{(1+|t_{1}-t_{2}|)^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}B\sqrt{d}}{T_{k}}+\frac{C_{1}d}{T_{k}}\leq C_{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}^{\top}\overline{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T_{k}}+\frac{Cd}{T_{k}B^{\alpha-1}}+C\leq C_{1}.

(B.23)

From (
B.21
), (
B.22
), and (
B.23
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t,k}-\vartheta_{t,k}\right\|_{M/2}\leq CB_{1}.

(B.24)

From (
B.19
) and (
B.24
),

\displaystyle\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\left\|\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)T_{k}\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}B_{1}\leq C_{1}H\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}B_{1}^{3/2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)T_{k}\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}B_{1}\leq C_{1}H\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}B_{1}^{3/2}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right)\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}-u}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1}+u,k}-\vartheta_{t_{1}+u,k}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{u=0}^{T_{k}-B-1}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{u}{H}\right)T_{k}B^{2}_{1}\leq C_{1}H\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B^{2}_{1}.

From these observations, we have

\displaystyle\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\left(\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}-\vartheta_{t_{1},k}\vartheta_{t_{2},k}\right)\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(B.25)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B_{1}d}H\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{3/2}B^{3/2}_{1}+\frac{C}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B_{1}d}H\mathcal{T}_{\circ}B^{2}_{1}\leq\frac{C_{1}H\sqrt{B_{1}}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V^{2}_{k}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\right.

(B.26)

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{C}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\kappa_{2}/2}}+\frac{CH\sqrt{B_{1}}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\kappa_{2}/2}}.

From (
B.26
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)-\mathrm{Var}\left(\zeta\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=2}^{K}\left|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V_{k}^{2}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},k}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},k}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{k}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},k}\right]\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle+(K-1)\left|\frac{4\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}{V_{1}^{2}d}\sum_{t_{1}=B+1}^{T_{1}}\sum_{t_{2}=B+1}^{T_{k}}\mathcal{K}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{H}\right)\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{1},1}\widehat{\vartheta}_{t_{2},1}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}(B_{1}-B)}}{V_{1}\sqrt{d}}\sum_{B\leq|t_{1}-t_{2}|\leq B_{1}}^{T_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{1},1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t_{2},1}\right]\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\kappa_{2}/2}}\right).

Choose
\psi=\sqrt{\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})},
then

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\zeta\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\zeta\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\zeta\right)\right]\right|+\mathbf{Pr}\left(x-\frac{1}{\psi}\leq\zeta\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\zeta\right)\right]\right|+\frac{C}{\psi},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{Pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\zeta\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\zeta\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq-\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\zeta\right)\right]\right|-\mathbf{Pr}\left(x-\frac{1}{\psi}\leq\zeta\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\zeta\right)\right]\right|-\frac{C}{\psi}.

Since
\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}
has normal distribution conditional on observations, from Assumption 4,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}(\zeta)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\psi\left|\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)}-\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\zeta)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\psi\left|\mathrm{Var}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\right)-\mathrm{Var}(\zeta)\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\log^{3/2}(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{1/6-\kappa_{2}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}}{\mathcal{T}_{\circ}^{\kappa_{2}/2}}\right),

so

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{Pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{S}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{Pr}\left(\zeta\leq x\right)\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(\mathcal{T}_{\circ})}}\right),

which proves (
22
).
∎
